Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in power theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 34 and the invariant I(A,B) = 122 mod 3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to power structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 34/122 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 212.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on power structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 702. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 997 with relations in degree 3.
